# ROM Analysis Index

One line per game that has been analyzed. Reports live next to their ROM at
`roms/<Game Folder>/<Game Name>.md`.

| Dave Mirra Freestyle BMX 2 | No password system and no cheat/debug menu (PASSWORD/CODE/DEBUG/SECRET/UNLOCK = 0; CHEAT appears only in the trick-score rating table), but one unpublished pause-screen button code: Left, Right, Left, Right, Up, Down, L, R at 0x08027174 with matcher 0x08015C94/CDC and pause screen 0x080085C8, whose callback 0x080085B8 stores 0xFFFF into 0x03001608; a whole-ROM scan shows the matcher has only two callers (the pause screen and the 97-entry trick-input engine), so exactly one menu button code exists; entered on the real pause screen and RAM-verified (0x03001608 0x0000 -> 0xFFFF), with wrong last step, 7-step prefix and the same code on the main menu all leaving 0x0000, and extra keys such as Select tolerated; the published console master code is absent from this ROM in all mask encodings and only navigates menus; the latch is write-only (all four Thumb accesses are boot-clear, the pause callback and a bit-3 OR from the ten-star objective path) with read-watchpoints on every screen never firing, so it is a dead developer latch; menus enumerated and RAM-verified (main, ProQuest, rider select, mode select, level select, options, session pause, ProQuest pause) | Complete; the pause code's latch 0x03001608 is written but never read in this build, so it has no observable in-game effect | [report](Dave%20Mirra%20Freestyle%20BMX%202/Dave%20Mirra%20Freestyle%20BMX%202.md) |

| DemiKids: Dark Version | Password feature is a closed set of five literal answers in the script bank, each on exactly one hidden NPC with one attempt and a unique X joins your party message: Atlus/Chot at 0x082454A2, Light/Ikonda at 0x082455D0, Megami/Pandora at 0x08245610, Tensei/Starhair at 0x0824570A, Dark/Lucifroz at 0x0824593E; a full decoded-script scan finds the five answers once each, the five success messages once each and no sixth block, with no checksum or generator, so the accepted set is exactly those five (all published); the engine uses a non-ASCII codec (0x81 <code>, A-Z = 7C-96 with 7F reserved, a-z = 97-B0, space 81 40, controls FF/FE/F9), confirmed by decoding dialogue and by the in-game name buffer at 0x030010A0 (81 7C x8 for AAAAAAAA); input path is the shared 8-char dialog routine 0x08034084 with the Enter Password label at 0x082790FC, also used for Input Name (case-fold aliases such as ATLUS/atlus are derived candidate unpublished spellings); dev leftovers include a save-disk manager, Listen to BGM sound test and View Map/Power Unit/Switch Location/Access Vinecenter labels at 0x277A00-0x279600, not exposed in retail menus | Complete; the five NPCs are story-gated (earliest after the Wind Shrine) so no answer could be typed at an NPC in this run; the closed set is proven statically | [report](DemiKids%3A%20Dark%20Version/DemiKids%3A%20Dark%20Version.md) |

| Desert Strike Advance | The GBA image embeds a complete 2 MiB SNES game at file 0x200000 whose 65C816 code holds the password logic (addresses = image offsets); a 7-character CAMPAIGN MENU/ENTER PASSWORD screen is reached by holding A on the main menu, over the 26-symbol validator alphabet ZQMTH3L2J8RKBW94FP5SCNGX7D (the keypad offers 29 but V, 1 and 6 always fail), with checksum (sum ASCII c0..c5 - 390) mod 26 == index(c6) and field rules giving campaign (idx(c0)-idx(c5))/2+1 plus four decimal score digits; accepted space is exactly 40,000 generic codes (4 campaigns x 10,000 scores) plus 4 hard-coded specials out of 29^7 = 17,249,876,309 UI strings; novel generic RWJJKHG entered live gives CAMPAIGN #4 NUCLEAR STORM with SCORE 5003000 (RAM $020018BC=3, $02002070=05 00 00 03, score 0x004C56F8), and novel specials B84GT7S ($20FE=1), B74GT7S ($2100=1) and GTSTFTC ($217C=1000) were entered; published 3ZJMZT7 campaign 2, W3HWK8B campaign 3 and BS9JS27 LIVES 10 (special-cased, fails the generic checksum) verified; RWJJKHB (bad check char) and RFFFPZC (checksum-valid but campaign field 5) both rejected with RAM unchanged | Complete | [report](Desert%20Strike%20Advance/Desert%20Strike%20Advance.md) |
| Dexter's Laboratory: Chess Challenge | Only cheat surface is OPTIONS -> PASSWORD: 16 cells over the 16-symbol alphabet BCDFGHJKLMNPQRST (one 4-bit nibble per cell, 64 bits, nominal 16^16 ~ 1.84e19; buffer 16xu32 at IWRAM 0x03007A88 with cells 1-8 showing words 8-15 and 9-16 showing words 0-7); input code 0x08022548 (inc, wrap 15->0) and 0x080225CC (dec, wrap 0->15), key state ~KEYINPUT&0x3FF at 0x02000A48; results are PASSWORD ACTIVE (decoding player 1/2, tier, side, level) or PASSWORD DENIED; all 30 published level codes are algorithmic rather than literal (no packed copy exists) and levels 02 (HQJSJJBP) and 30 (BLRNKHHM) were loaded and accepted in-game as DEXTER/DEE DEE/TIER 1/WHITE/EASY; the novel code CSHJMQNSKTGRDHFN, generated by the game's own encoder for a fresh save and absent from every published list, was submitted and confirmed PASSWORD ACTIVE, proving the 30 are not the whole set; rejection bounds: all 16 single-nibble +1 mutations of a valid code plus three compensating two-nibble pairs return PASSWORD DENIED, so the whole 64 bits are integrity-checked, and values >15 are unreachable because the handler wraps; dead strings from the sibling engine (FIXED ALL MACHINES, LOW GRAVITY, START LIVES, MAP/JUMP/PUNCH) and the unproven YOU HAVE UNLOCKED/BOY GENIUS MODE/PUZZLE MODE lead | Complete | [report](Dexter%27s%20Laboratory%3A%20Chess%20Challenge/Dexter%27s%20Laboratory%3A%20Chess%20Challenge.md) |

| Dinotopia: The Timestone Pirates | ROM is a patched Back from the Dead image (entry branches to an appended cracktro at 0x08765400 with banner at 0x08768EFB and rebuilt header), analysed from a copy with only the 4-byte entry restored; both published GBA codes are real and verified verbatim in the ROM at 0x0850FE60/0x0850FE6A - U U D D L R A L R B opens level select then Start -> Credits -> farm with numbered doors, and D D U U R L B R L A is the stage skip (pause + Select); the space is closed: matcher 0x08031DE0 tracks 6 buttons x 10 presses = 60,466,176 sequences of which exactly 2 are accepted, and a whole-ROM referenced key-mask scan found no other input table, with no password alphabet or screen in any of 5 languages (0 keyword hits in 81,048 strings); unpublished effects found and RAM-verified: with debug flag 0x030005C8 set, pause R adds lives at 0x030005BC clamping at 100 and pause Select advances one scene record (0x0803FECC -> 0x0803FEF0), both no-ops without the flag, and holding L+R+Select while typing either code is still accepted because the matcher ignores unmasked buttons (an alias, not a new code); one-wrong-button sequences leave both flags 0 and credits without the code return to the main menu rather than the farm | Complete | [report](Dinotopia%3A%20The%20Timestone%20Pirates/Dinotopia%3A%20The%20Timestone%20Pirates.md) |
| Disney Princess | Image is a scene release (header checksum zeroed, entry 0xEA1CDDF9 boots a SUNHORS trainer with UNL. ENERGY/UNL. WEAPONS/IMMORTAL/NO TIMER/LEVELSKIP), analysed from a copy with the entry restored, and the trainer is reported separately; the real system is a 20-symbol password (Table of Contents -> Password) over the 50-symbol alphabet BCDFGHJKLMNPQRSTVWXZbcdfghjklmnpqrstvwxz0123456789 at 0x0832377E, buffer 0x030065D0, validator 0x08074D60, decoder 0x08003148, encoder 0x08002FF8+0x08074854; algorithm reversed as pairwise swaps plus XOR mask plus a differential mod-50 chain with a 2-symbol checksum (chk[1] = sum of even deltas mod 50, B[0]&0x1F = quotient), decoding to 11 status words at 0x03001858, a 19-bit flag word at 0x03001874 and 47 treasure bits at 0x030017E0; space 50^20 ~ 9.09e33 with ~7.6e30 valid (1 in 1194), about 5% of payloads unrepresentable and symbol 19 ignored giving 50 aliases per code; of the published list 21/23 verify, FlLC0kkL9FgWnW1FlRsZ and FvDMvtxV5pqwxwPfvr2z are rejected by the checksum and two others contain OCR O instead of 0; the special-stage gate is (flags & 0x3FFFC) == 0x3FFFC; novel code sw7003Fv648tJ7SZF2PB was minted with the ROM's own encoder and typed on the real keypad, unlocking Special Stage and Snow White 5/8 treasures (intro and gameplay reached), plus 11 further verified codes in 6b; one-character corruption stays on the Password screen (state 0x03001780=0x87), the published Aurora-1 code is rejected and a checksum-valid extreme payload is accepted (no range layer) | Complete | [report](Disney%20Princess/Disney%20Princess.md) |

| Disney Presents Home on the Range | Scene-patched release (Mode-7/GLENBEY iL Scn. 2004 cracktro with SABREC/ASPHIX/MOONLORD credits before the stock logo; header and entry stock, so data-level) with a real 4-symbol password whose validator was recovered at 0x08003204: cells are objects 2-5 of 0x02001590, symbol = [[obj+0x38]+0x2E] over the 16-symbol alphabet B C D F H J K L M N P Q R S T V (A unreachable), code = v0<<12|v1<<8|v2<<4|v3 validated by MT19937 (0x08012C40/84, 69069 init) seeded 0x29A + 2*bit15 + bit0, 40 seeded bit swaps (0x08002E80), then accept iff (MT(sgenrand(2*X)) & 0x7F) == B, (P>>1)&0xF <= 10 and (P>>10)&0x1F maps through 0x08002BA4/0x08002CA4 to a non-zero stage pointer in 0x0818F83C..0x08190B08; enumerating 16^4 = 65,536 inputs yields exactly 165 accepted codes (15 stage blocks x 11 sub-levels), all 14 published codes are members (one per block B..O, block A unpublished); in-game proof BBKD and DVHB show CORRECT, minted unpublished BTBB (0x0E00, block 0x0818FCA4, L=0) shows CORRECT, fades and starts the opening cutscene, while BTBC (one symbol off), 0x1234, BBBB and 0x0000 are rejected; method note: call 0x08003204 from the debugger with IME=0, r0=code, lr=idle loop as a ground-truth oracle | Complete | [report](Disney%20Presents%20Home%20on%20the%20Range/Disney%20Presents%20Home%20on%20the%20Range.md) |

| Donkey Kong Country | One developer cheat system: a 3-record button-code table at 0x0853DC00 with validator 0x08015C80, entered on the File Select screen while holding Select, with no password system, text-code validator or hidden level-select/debug screen across the fully walked 11-state menu dispatcher; codes verified in RAM and emulator: Ballad B,A,L,L,A,Down sets 0x0002 (Sound Player), Barrel B,A,R,R,A,L sets 0x0001 (50 lives) and Bubba B,Up,B,B,A sets 0x0004 (bonus/token areas); novel unpublished combinations proven: B,A,Right,Right,A,Left gives 0x0001 and B,A,Left,Left,A,Down gives 0x0002 because the L/R shoulders and the d-pad Left/Right map to the same stored masks; an offline reimplementation of the validator enumerated every stored-mask sequence of length 1-6 (299,592 inputs) finding exactly 11 accepted stored sequences (3 families plus 8 Bubba variants from a missing exact-length check) and 23 accepted physical spellings of length <=6 with 20 absent from published lists; a separate hidden combo L+R+Start+A jumps to the erase-save-data confirmation, name entry is a profile label rather than a password field, SNES-version codes were rejected, and bounds were proven by wrong-button, truncated, leading-button and title-screen rejections | Complete | [report](Donkey%20Kong%20Country/Donkey%20Kong%20Country.md) |
| Dokapon: Monster Hunter | No password/cheat/menu-code system: no entry screen, validator, alphabet or hard-coded codes, and all published codes for the SKU are GameShark/CodeBreaker/AR memory hacks (out of scope, not ROM literals); text is not ASCII but 16-bit font codes (lowercase = ASCII+0x1A, uppercase = ASCII-0x0B, space 0x0001, 0xFFFF terminator, 0x83xx/0x90xx inline tags), and decoding 4,194,304 words / 10,428 strings gives password/cheat/code/debug/bonus = 0 hits; screens walked include title, main menu, file select, intro, guild test, field, pause tabs (item/arms/change/system) and the system submenu (Diary save, Flash, Mss, Album, Skill), with no name-entry keyboard despite the narrative write-your-name line; a genuine 7-category debug menu exists at 0x08007A1C (Item/Char-btl/Scenario/Event/W-comp/S-comp/Sound) with Item page counter 1/190 and names Recover1/Recover2/Energy1..., gated by save-header halfword 0x6264 at 0x03004C60+0x0A and Select in menu state 1, but no ROM code writes 0x6264 (boot sanitizer 0x08006574 preserves it) and retail input never sets state 1, so it is documented via a debugger jump and labelled as instrumentation; enumeration gives space sizes, string and button counts (no >=4-run mask table in code) and the title/menu combo rejection matrix | Complete; the 7-category debug menu is only reachable by writing the save-header magic and forcing menu state 1, both documented as instrumentation | [report](Dokapon%3A%20Monster%20Hunter/Dokapon%3A%20Monster%20Hunter.md) |
| Disney's Lilo & Stitch | MODE7 release (entry 0xEA1FE5FE -> cracktro loader at 0x087F9800, intro text at 0x7FD2FA, no trainer menu), analysed from a copy with only the stock entry restored (3 bytes differ); main finding is a 7-icon password system over 8 icons (Stitch, Lilo, Scrump, Gun, UFO, Flower, Rocket, Pineapple) at 3 bits each, bit-sliced into lives(3), f6(3), mode(3) and flags(8) plus a 4-bit checksum ~(lives+f6+mode+flags)&0xF, with decoder 0x08001FB0, encoder 0x08001E9C, screens 0x08010E70/0x08010F28 and buffer 0x03007D70; accepted set is 131,073 of 2,097,152 (131,072 checksum-valid plus the all-Stitch special case), f6 has no gameplay reader giving 8 aliases, and the flags high nibble does not affect the checksum; novel codes entered and RAM-verified: Flower,Lilo,Lilo,Scrump,UFO,UFO,Stitch (bytes 05 01 01 02 04 04 00) gives lives 8, f6 1, mode 1 Mothership, flags 1 with RAM 0x201140=8, 0x200A48=1, 0x200A4C=1, 0x201144=1, 0x200E70=3, 0x206154=4 and an x8 HUD, and Flower,Lilo,Lilo,Scrump,UFO,UFO,Scrump is accepted as the flags 0x81 alias, while Flower,Lilo,Lilo,Gun,UFO,UFO,Scrump is rejected as INCORRECT with buffer 05 01 01 03 04 04 02; published Beach (all-Stitch), Mothership, Lilo x7 and Unlock All (state 15, flags 0xFF to credits then all 4 movies and the 8-picture gallery) verified; a 99-lives button code at 0x08001686 (KEYINPUT & 0x104, sets 0x02001140=99) was verified by RAM and HUD | Complete | [report](Disney%27s%20Lilo%20%26%20Stitch/Disney%27s%20Lilo%20%26%20Stitch.md) |
| Digimon Racing | No password, cheat-code or button-code system and no reachable debug menu — bounded: UI text is pre-rendered 8x8 font tiles rather than ASCII and 22 keywords x 6 encodings over all 8 MiB plus 733 LZ77 blocks (146,383 B) give 0 genuine hits; only two real KEYINPUT reads exist (0x0800889C input builder, 0x0801194C time-trial recorder, the other two 0x04000130 literals being CpuSet control words) with no mask-sequence validator and all 34 button-shaped runs disassembling to code/data; 21 interactive menu entries enumerated (main 4, game modes 4, options 8 with music/sfx/difficulty 3/HUD 2, char-select 3, pause 2, language 5, trophy room 9, both link screens); roster index 0x0300547C is an 11-position ring with 10 distinct racers (7 base + 3 locked SECRET RACER slots) whose in-ROM gate text (win all time trials / all quick races / all cup races) differs from published lists; four tracks (DESERT OASIS, TOY TOWN, DESERT CLIFFS, SWAMP BOG) and WESTERN CUP; save format decoded as 512-byte EEPROM with a 24-byte record, magic DRGBA7, 8 option bytes and a 16-bit two's-complement checksum (mGBA .sav blocks byte-reversed), with the 232-byte profile block the progress store and unlock bits never observed being written (marked unknown); header checksum field zeroed (expected 0xFA57) as a rebuild artifact | Complete | [report](Digimon%20Racing/Digimon%20Racing.md) |
| Demon Driver: Time to Burn Rubber | Real 20-character password system over the 32-symbol alphabet WRTYPSDFGHJKLZXCVBNM0123456789-#: fixed 20-byte key at 0x083B0ED4, keyed 5-bit transform, 84-bit payload with 14 bounded fields and a 16-bit checksum; validator 0x0800AE00, input handler 0x0800D8B0, field table 0x08055E54; space is 32^20 raw (2^100), ~2^84 checksum-valid, 216,628,510,531,082,004,725,760 semantic field states; novel code NHLYCZYCCWCXGDY#YM0M typed in-game (buffer read back exactly) and RAM-verified to SELECT TRACK / CHINA / GREAT WALL RUN with 0x03005051=0x1F, 0x03004EBC=0x03 and 0x03004EC0=0x0B; the game-generated 0HLYCZYCCWCXGDY#YT0M decodes and re-encodes exactly and re-entry gives FRANCE / CITY LIMITS; the published GameFAQs code MHXZUZYCCWFJ4RMTY81M is rejected because it contains U outside this ROM's alphabet, is absent from the image and shows INVALID PASSWORD; field-1 overflow code 09LYCZYCCWCXGDY#Y00M and corrupted-checksum NHLYCZYCCWCXGDY#YM0W are both rejected on-screen; save type is password and no debug/cheat strings exist, with the password screen the only code surface | Complete | [report](Demon%20Driver%3A%20Time%20to%20Burn%20Rubber/Demon%20Driver%3A%20Time%20to%20Burn%20Rubber.md) |
| Defender of the Crown | No in-game password/code/unlock system — bounded: PASSWORD/CHEAT/UNLOCK/SCENARIO/CODE/OPTION/HIDDEN/BONUS all 0 across the 4 MiB (DEBUG=1 is the dead HORSE DEBUG string, TEST=3 are battle test labels, SECRET=2 is story prose), no name-entry alphabet (the only 77-entry table at 0x0801C1A0 is the renderer glyph order) and the 191-string UI bank holds no code list or keypad legend, with progress in a 512-byte battery save; all menus walked and RAM-verified (6-item main menu, 4-knight scenario select Cedric/Geoffrey/Wolfric/Wilfred, enemy skill EASY/MEDIUM/HARD at 0x0300385C, 4-item pause with music 0x030011D4 and SFX 0x03003448); two dead developer screens fully enumerated - a 12-entry test menu at 0x08009B64 (table 0x08009D2C) and a 5-parameter HORSE DEBUG at 0x08012F94 - with zero bl/blx/b/bcc/ARM-bl/pointer/ADR references across 4 MiB and live breakpoints never firing while the pause control breakpoint did; published GBA codes are CodeBreaker memory patches (gold 0x03001150, artillery 0x03000FB6/8/BA) verified as addresses but out of scope; Amiga hold-K and H+J+K+L analogues tested with no state change | Complete | [report](Defender%20of%20the%20Crown/Defender%20of%20the%20Crown.md) |
| Defender | No password, button code or code-entry screen (zero hits for password/passcode/cheat/secret/unlock; zero key-mask sequences in u16 held, active-low and classic UUDDLRLRBA forms), but a hidden DEBUG MENU exists as page 6 of the credits/attract carousel: text block at 0x080811BC (13 rows x 31 B, 9 options PSWD DESC, INFINITE LIVES, INFINITE BOMBS, STOLEN MOJO, GOOFY HATS, FUNNY, GOOD LOOKING, BULLETPROOF, INVISIBILITY) with handler 0x0800CD54, dispatcher 0x0800E736 and table 0x0800E77C; the gate is a ROM constant rather than input (movs r2,#0 at 0x08006F6C feeding RAM 0x03007A88), so retail count at 0x03007A8C is 6 and the L/R ring only reaches states 0-5, while forcing count to 7 reaches state 6 and VRAM 0x0600E200 decodes to DEBUG MENU plus all nine rows (text-only proof); page 6 is inert (A/B/Start leave state and flag bytes unchanged) and is therefore a shipped developer leftover reachable only by ROM/RAM patching, not an in-game cheat; no codes published (VGChartz lists point values only) | Complete | [report](Defender/Defender.md) |
| Daredevil | One 11-character password system over the 32-symbol alphabet #BCDFGHJKLMNPQRSTVWXYZ0123456789 (the # is the DD head icon that sites print as @/¤/[DD]; alphabet at 0x0802FB30/51/74), 5 bits per char into 55 bits with a bijective permutation dst[(s*29) mod 55]=src[s], 47-bit payload plus an 8-bit checksum = sum(payload 6 bytes) mod 256; fields are map id-20 (bits 17-21), score/50 (16 bits), f6 (22-25), f4 (26) and a 15-bit secrets mask (32-46), with bits 27-31 and 0 unused giving 64 spellings per payload; validator 0x0801DD50/0x0801D5F4 and keypad handler 0x0801CC88 with buffer 0x03006570, counter 0x0300657C and decoded record 0x03005290; all 23 published level passwords and 41TK1S6ZNGV re-encode exactly, and the published RL#7S#RXHKM turns out to decode to map 25 duplicating A Question Of Honor while maps 26 and 34 are missing from the published set; novel unpublished PNP#8#RBC#N (map 34, score 119000, f6=9, flags 0x1F) was entered on the in-game PASSWORD screen and RAM-verified as record 00 00 FF FF 00 22 09 00 D8 D0 01 00, with a second spelling FMFBM#MB##B generated; Y#PSC##K##5 (same payload, bad checksum 125 vs 109) is rejected with record zero and the entry screen retained; PNB6B8#6BC#N decodes map 46 with no semantic clamp at 0x2D | Complete | [report](Daredevil/Daredevil.md) |
| Danny Phantom: Urban Jungle | This SKU has no code system — bounded: 0 hits for PASSWORD/CHEAT/SECRET/DEBUG/CODE/INVALID/WRONG/ERROR/ACCEPT/OPTION, 0 tables of three or more 4-uppercase-letter NUL-padded records, 0 published literals present under ASCII/lower/+0x20/UTF-16/32/reversed/255 single-byte XOR-add-sub/base-26/5-bit packing (the eight ECTO hits are substrings of DIRECTOR, in the credits), and no validator or character-input consumer (of 110 literal loads of the joypad global 0x03001D50 the only exact-combination gate is the A+B+Select+Start soft reset at 0x08000520 via thunk 0x08085F20); the three-item start menu (NEW GAME / GAME DIFFICULTY / GHOST GALLERY) has no Passwords item and was verified as a 3-ring by OAM (Down 0->1->2->0, Up 0->2->1) with each destination mapped by DISPCNT/BGxCNT and RAM counters; the published RUSH/VLAD/JAZZ/DASH/ECTO/SEEK codes belong to the sibling SKU The Ultimate Enemy (AGB-BUEE), and the two builds differ in engine (joypad global 0x03001D50 here vs 0x03001F60 there); unlockables are save-based (gallery/difficulty) | Complete | [report](Danny%20Phantom%3A%20Urban%20Jungle/Danny%20Phantom%3A%20Urban%20Jungle.md) |
| Danny Phantom: The Ultimate Enemy | Passwords screen is screen-module id 4 (record 0x08112854, init 0x0802A32C, update 0x0802A450, validator 0x0802A9BC), reached boot -> title 17 -> 19 -> start menu 2 -> Start -> item 1 -> Options 3 -> item 1 -> Passwords 4; it is a 3x11 background-tile picker (26 letter cells A-K/L-U/V-Z plus 7 function cells) whose buffer is hard-capped at 4 by cmp count,#3 before validate, so the raw space is 33^4 = 1,185,921 cell sequences (26^4 letter-only) and the validator is a chain of six exact 8-dword comparisons, making the accepted set exactly {JAZZ, SEEK, DASH, ECTO, VLAD, RUSH} at blocks 0x0802AA98/AB18/AB90/AC08/AC80/ACF8; all six were typed on the real screen and accepted, 0x08000ED8(k) setting 0x03001CCA+k for k<=3 and 0x03001CD1/0x03001DF4 for k>3 (JAZZ->id 6 then 9, SEEK/DASH/ECTO->6, VLAD->2, RUSH->13 then 0); rejection observed for DASA and AAAA (stay on id 4, buffer 4->0) and a fifth letter is never committed because it is consumed by validate(); the published 5-7 letter codes (ENEMY, FRIGHT, PHANTOM, GHOST, GHOUL, HAUNT, FENTON, PORTAL, DANNY) are not enterable here: whole-ROM scans found no second letter widget and no second table in the cell encoding, nor any text/packed/affine/hash encoding of those words, and the Options mode flag does not change the cap, so the public FAQ claims are likely DS-version contamination | Complete; the 4-letter space is closed and verified in-game, while the 5-7 letter published codes are proven unenterable in this build and their true host SKU is not identified | [report](Danny%20Phantom%3A%20The%20Ultimate%20Enemy/Danny%20Phantom%3A%20The%20Ultimate%20Enemy.md) |

Status values: `Complete`, or `Complete; <specific unprovable item>` where evidence genuinely ends. No "in progress" or "partial" placeholders.
